- 4647 files · ~10,766,636 words

- 64941 nodes · 187475 edges · 1314 communities (1191 shown, 123 thin omitted)

- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
